  select_effect = { hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = ctc_cp_ITA_left_5_9_started country_event = { id = ctc_cp_ITA.3100 days = 28 } } }
  completion_reward = { custom_effect_tooltip = ctc_cp_completion_tt hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ctc_cp_ITA_left_5_9_answered } } country_event = { id = ctc_cp_ITA.3100 days = 1 } } country_event = { id = ctc_cp_ITA.3102 days = 7 } } }
 }
}
